[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule


[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 2/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h0E02) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 1"); end
    a = 16'h8003; b = 16'h6479; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h8AB6) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 2"); end
    a = 16'h7521; b = 16'h0003; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h1BB2) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 3"); end

    for (int sa = 0; sa < 2048; sa++) begin
      a = {sa[10], 5'b00000, sa[9:0]};
      for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin b = structured[i]; check(); end
    end
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin
      a = structured[i];
      for (int sb = 0; sb < 2048; sb++) begin b = {sb[10], 5'b00000, sb[9:0]}; check(); end
    end
    ra = 16'h0001; rb = 16'hBEEF;
    for (int k = 0; k < (1 << 21); k++) begin
      ra = lfsr_next(ra); rb = lfsr_next(lfsr_next(rb));
      a = ra; b = rb; check();
    end

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total, errors);
    if (errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // Small leading-zero encoders for the two 11-bit significands\n  reg [3:0] lz_a, lz_b;\n  always @* begin\n    casez (m_a)\n      11'b1??????????: lz_a = 4'd0;\n      11'b01?????????: lz_a = 4'd1;\n      11'b001????????: lz_a = 4'd2;\n      11'b0001???????: lz_a = 4'd3;\n      11'b00001??????: lz_a = 4'd4;\n      11'b000001?????: lz_a = 4'd5;\n      11'b0000001????: lz_a = 4'd6;\n      11'b00000001???: lz_a = 4'd7;\n      11'b000000001??: lz_a = 4'd8;\n      11'b0000000001?: lz_a = 4'd9;\n      11'b00000000001: lz_a = 4'd10;\n      default:         lz_a = 4'd11;\n    endcase\n    casez (m_b)\n      11'b1??????????: lz_b = 4'd0;\n      11'b01?????????: lz_b = 4'd1;\n      11'b001????????: lz_b = 4'd2;\n      11'b0001???????: lz_b = 4'd3;\n      11'b00001??????: lz_b = 4'd4;\n      11'b000001?????: lz_b = 4'd5;\n      11'b0000001????: lz_b = 4'd6;\n      11'b00000001???: lz_b = 4'd7;\n      11'b000000001??: lz_b = 4'd8;\n      11'b0000000001?: lz_b = 4'd9;\n      11'b00000000001: lz_b = 4'd10;\n      default:         lz_b = 4'd11;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  wire [4:0] s_lz = {1'b0, lz_a} + {1'b0, lz_b};\n  wire [4:0] top_idx = 5'd21 - s_lz;\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // Small leading-zero encoders for the two 11-bit significands\n  reg [3:0] lz_a, lz_b;\n  always @* begin\n    lz_a = (m_a[10]) ? 4'd0 :\n           (m_a[9])  ? 4'd1 :\n           (m_a[8])  ? 4'd2 :\n           (m_a[7])  ? 4'd3 :\n           (m_a[6])  ? 4'd4 :\n           (m_a[5])  ? 4'd5 :\n           (m_a[4])  ? 4'd6 :\n           (m_a[3])  ? 4'd7 :\n           (m_a[2])  ? 4'd8 :\n           (m_a[1])  ? 4'd9 :\n           (m_a[0])  ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n    lz_b = (m_b[10]) ? 4'd0 :\n           (m_b[9])  ? 4'd1 :\n           (m_b[8])  ? 4'd2 :\n           (m_b[7])  ? 4'd3 :\n           (m_b[6])  ? 4'd4 :\n           (m_b[5])  ? 4'd5 :\n           (m_b[4])  ? 4'd6 :\n           (m_b[3])  ? 4'd7 :\n           (m_b[2])  ? 4'd8 :\n           (m_b[1])  ? 4'd9 :\n           (m_b[0])  ? 4'd10 : 4'd11;\n  end\n\n  wire [4:0] s_lz = {1'b0, lz_a} + {1'b0, lz_b};\n  wire [4:0] top_idx = 5'd21 - s_lz;\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [4:0] lz = zero_in ? 5'd0 : (bit_at(prod, top_idx) ? s_lz : s_lz + 5'd1);\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod, sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  // exponent of a normal result\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // subnormal result path\n  integer          total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod, total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  // pack\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [4:0] lz = zero_in ? 5'd0 : (bit_at(prod, top_idx) ? s_lz : s_lz + 5'd1);\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // Normal result path: for every normal (or overflowing) selectable case the\n  // leading one must be shifted right onto bit 10 (lz <= 11).  Non-selected\n  // underflow cases may place arbitrary values in these wires.\n  wire [4:0]        sr        = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted   = prod >> sr;\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre  = shifted[10:0];\n  wire              guard     = bit_at(prod, sr - 1);\n  wire              sticky    = bit_at(below, sr - 2);\n  wire              round_up  = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // subnormal result path\n  integer          total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod, total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  // pack\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0] eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  // Leading-zero count of the raw fraction (only meaningful for subnormals).\n  function [3:0] lzc10(input [9:0] v);\n    begin\n      lzc10 = (v[9]) ? 4'd0 :\n              (v[8]) ? 4'd1 :\n              (v[7]) ? 4'd2 :\n              (v[6]) ? 4'd3 :\n              (v[5]) ? 4'd4 :\n              (v[4]) ? 4'd5 :\n              (v[3]) ? 4'd6 :\n              (v[2]) ? 4'd7 :\n              (v[1]) ? 4'd8 :\n              (v[0]) ? 4'd9 : 4'd10;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [3:0] lzc_a = lzc10(f_a);\n  wire [3:0] lzc_b = lzc10(f_b);\n\n  // Shift needed to make a subnormal significand's leading one sit on bit 10.\n  wire [4:0] extra_a = e_a_zero ? ({1'b0, lzc_a} + 5'd1) : 5'd0;\n  wire [4:0] extra_b = e_b_zero ? ({1'b0, lzc_b} + 5'd1) : 5'd0;\n\n  // Pre-normalized 11-bit significands.\n  wire [10:0] raw_a = {1'b0, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] raw_b = {1'b0, f_b};\n  wire [10:0] m_a_n = e_a_zero ? (raw_a << extra_a) : {1'b1, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b_n = e_b_zero ? (raw_b << extra_b) : {1'b1, f_b};\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a_n * m_b_n;\n\n  // Product of two normalized significands is in [2^20, 2^22).\n  wire [4:0] extrasum = {1'b0, extra_a} + {1'b0, extra_b};\n  wire       lz       = prod[21] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] lz_orig  = zero_in ? 5'd0 : (extrasum + lz);\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // Normal-product rounding: shift right by 11 if bit21 set, else by 10.\n  wire              lz_is0    = (lz == 5'd0);\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted   = lz_is0 ? (prod >> 5'd11) : (prod >> 5'd10);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre  = shifted[10:0];\n  wire              guard     = lz_is0 ? bit_at(prod, 5'd10) : bit_at(prod, 5'd9);\n  wire              sticky    = lz_is0 ? bit_at(below, 5'd9) : bit_at(below, 5'd8);\n  wire              round_up  = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz_orig};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz_orig};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // Subnormal path: shift normalized product by q = 26 - exp_sum + extrasum.\n  integer          q;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    q = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) + $signed({1'b0, extrasum});\n    sub_shifted = (q >= 0 && q < PROD_W) ? (prod >> q) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod, q - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, q - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  function [4:0] lzc22;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] p;\n    reg [4:0] val;\n    begin\n      if (|p[21:12]) begin\n        if (|p[21:17]) begin\n          if (p[21])      val = 5'd0;\n          else if (p[20]) val = 5'd1;\n          else if (p[19]) val = 5'd2;\n          else if (p[18]) val = 5'd3;\n          else            val = 5'd4; // p[17]\n        end else begin\n          if (p[16])      val = 5'd5;\n          else if (p[15]) val = 5'd6;\n          else if (p[14]) val = 5'd7;\n          else if (p[13]) val = 5'd8;\n          else            val = 5'd9; // p[12]\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (|p[11:6]) begin\n          if (p[11])      val = 5'd10;\n          else if (p[10]) val = 5'd11;\n          else if (p[9])  val = 5'd12;\n          else if (p[8])  val = 5'd13;\n          else if (p[7])  val = 5'd14;\n          else            val = 5'd15; // p[6]\n        end else begin\n          if (p[6])       val = 5'd16; // already covered by p[11:6] group bound; harmless\n          else if (p[5])  val = 5'd17;\n          else if (p[4])  val = 5'd18;\n          else if (p[3])  val = 5'd19;\n          else if (p[2])  val = 5'd20;\n          else if (p[1])  val = 5'd21;\n          else            val = 5'd0;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc22 = val;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [4:0] lz = lzc22(prod);\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod, sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  integer          total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0] sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  // Shared logarithmic right shifter with sticky accumulation.\n  // d = prod >> sh_amt, sticky = OR of the low sh_amt bits of prod.\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sum   = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0]  total_shift8 = 8'd26 - {3'd0, exp_sum};\n  wire [4:0]  total_shift  = total_shift8[4:0];\n  wire        q_gt0        = (total_shift8 != 8'd0);\n\n  wire need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0] sr   = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [4:0] sl   = lz - 5'd11;\n\n  // Normal-path right shift places the first discarded bit in d[0]:\n  // sh_amt = sr-1 = 10-lz.\n  wire [4:0] n_norm = 5'd10 - lz;\n  wire [4:0] n_sub  = total_shift - 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] sh_amt = need_right ? n_norm : n_sub; // chosen only by final selection below\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d0 = sh_amt[0] ? (prod >> 5'd1)  : prod;\n  wire              s0 = sh_amt[0] ? prod[0] : 1'b0;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d1 = sh_amt[1] ? (d0 >> 5'd2)    : d0;\n  wire              s1 = s0 | (sh_amt[1] ? |d0[1:0] : 1'b0);\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d2 = sh_amt[2] ? (d1 >> 5'd4)    : d1;\n  wire              s2 = s1 | (sh_amt[2] ? |d1[3:0] : 1'b0);\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d3 = sh_amt[3] ? (d2 >> 5'd8)    : d2;\n  wire              s3 = s2 | (sh_amt[3] ? |d2[7:0] : 1'b0);\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d4 = sh_amt[4] ? (d3 >> 5'd16)   : d3;\n  wire              s4 = s3 | (sh_amt[4] ? |d3[15:0] : 1'b0);\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] rshifted = d4;\n  wire              rstick   = s4;\n\n  // Normal result significand before round (split left/right paths)\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] lshifted  = prod << sl;\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre  = need_right ? rshifted[11:1] : lshifted[10:0];\n  wire              guard     = need_right ? rshifted[0] : 1'b0;\n  wire              sticky    = need_right ? rstick : 1'b0;\n  wire              round_up  = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // Subnormal path, reusing the right-shifter when q > 0.\n  wire [9:0] frac_trunc = q_gt0 ? rshifted[10:1] : prod[9:0];\n  wire       guard_s    = q_gt0 ? rshifted[0] : 1'b0;\n  wire       sticky_s   = q_gt0 ? rstick : 1'b0;\n  wire       round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] q3 = total_shift[3] ? (q2 >> 5'd8)    : q2;
  wire              u3 = u2 | (total_shift[3] ? |q2[7:0] : 1'b0);
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] q4 = total_shift[4] ? (q3 >> 5'd16)   : q3;
  wire              u4 = u3 | (total_shift[4] ? |q3[15:0] : 1'b0);

  wire [9:0] frac_trunc = q_gt0 ? q4[10:1] : prod[9:0];
  wire       guard_s    = q_gt0 ? q4[0] : 1'b0;
  wire       sticky_s   = q_gt0 ? u4 : 1'b0;
  wire       round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

// fp_mul_e5f10 -- starting-point datapath, but guard/sticky are produced by
// a logarithmic right-shifter with sticky accumulation rather than per-bit
// prefix-OR/mux trees.
module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;

  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  // Normal-path logarithmic right shifter with sticky accumulation for the
  // normal result (guard is bit 10-lz of the product).
  wire [4:0]  exp_sum      = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0] sl   = lz - 5'd11;
  wire [4:0] sh_amt = 5'd10 - lz;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d0 = sh_amt[0] ? (prod >> 5'd1)  : prod;
  wire              s0 = sh_amt[0] ? prod[0] : 1'b0;
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d1 = sh_amt[1] ? (d0 >> 5'd2)    : d0;
  wire              s1 = s0 | (sh_amt[1] ? |d0[1:0] : 1'b0);
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d2 = sh_amt[2] ? (d1 >> 5'd4)    : d1;
  wire              s2 = s1 | (sh_amt[2] ? |d1[3:0] : 1'b0);
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d3 = sh_amt[3] ? (d2 >> 5'd8)    : d2;
  wire              s3 = s2 | (sh_amt[3] ? |d2[7:0] : 1'b0);
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d4 = sh_amt[4] ? (d3 >> 5'd16)   : d3;
  wire              s4 = s3 | (sh_amt[4] ? |d3[15:0] : 1'b0);

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] rshifted  = d4;

[Step 44/60]
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  // Normal-path logarithmic right shifter with sticky accumulation for the
  // normal result (guard is bit 10-lz of the product).
  wire [7:0]  exp_sum      = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0] sl   = lz - 5'd11;
  wire [4:0] sh_amt = 5'd10 - lz;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d0 = sh_amt[0] ? (prod >> 5'd1)  : prod;
  wire              s0 = sh_amt[0] ? prod[0] : 1'b0;
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d1 = sh_amt[1] ? (d0 >> 5'd2)    : d0;
  wire              s1 = s0 | (sh_amt[1] ? |d0[1:0] : 1'b0);
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d2 = sh_amt[2] ? (d1 >> 5'd4)    : d1;
  wire              s2 = s1 | (sh_amt[2] ? |d1[3:0] : 1'b0);
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d3 = sh_amt[3] ? (d2 >> 5'd8)    : d2;
  wire              s3 = s2 | (sh_amt[3] ? |d2[7:0] : 1'b0);
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d4 = sh_amt[4] ? (d3 >> 5'd16)   : d3;
  wire              s4 = s3 | (sh_amt[4] ? |d3[15:0] : 1'b0);

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] rshifted  = d4;
  wire              rstick    = s4;

  // Normal result significand before round (split left/right paths)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] lshifted  = prod << sl;
  wire              lz_eq_11  = (lz == 5'd11);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre  = need_right ? (lz_eq_11 ? prod[10:0] : rshifted[11:1]) : lshifted[10:0];
  wire              guard     = need_right ? (lz_eq_11 ? 1'b0 : rshifted[0]) : 1'b0;
  wire              sticky    = need_right ? (lz_eq_11 ? 1'b0 : rstick) : 1'b0;
  wire              round_up  = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);

  -> create_file(filename='fp_mul_v8.sv', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- v6 datapath, with normal and subnormal paths sharing one\n// logarithmic right-shifter/sticky accumulator.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum      = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire       need_right   = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire       lz_eq_11     = (lz == 5'd11);\n\n  // q = 26 - exp_sum; for subnormal results q is in 1..22.\n  wire [7:0] total_shift8 = 8'd26 - {3'd0, exp_sum};\n  wire [4:0] q_m1         = total_shift8[4:0] - 5'd1;\n\n  // One right-shifter performs:\n  //   normal case : shift by 10-lz (guard bit lands in d[0]; lz==11 is bypassed)\n  //   subnormal case: shift by q-1 (fraction becomes d[10:1], guard d[0]).\n  wire [4:0] n_shift = lz_eq_11 ? 5'd0 : (5'd10 - lz);\n  wire [4:0] sh_amt  = need_right ? n_shift : q_m1;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d0 = sh_amt[0] ? (prod >> 5'd1)  : prod;\n  wire              s0 = sh_amt[0] ? prod[0] : 1'b0;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d1 = sh_amt[1] ? (d0 >> 5'd2)    : d0;\n  wire              s1 = s0 | (sh_amt[1] ? |d0[1:0] : 1'b0);\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d2 = sh_amt[2] ? (d1 >> 5'd4)    : d1;\n  wire              s2 = s1 | (sh_amt[2] ? |d1[3:0] : 1'b0);\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d3 = sh_amt[3] ? (d2 >> 5'd8)    : d2;\n  wire              s3 = s2 | (sh_amt[3] ? |d2[7:0] : 1'b0);\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] d4 = sh_amt[4] ? (d3 >> 5'd16)   : d3;\n  wire              s4 = s3 | (sh_amt[4] ? |d3[15:0] : 1'b0);\n\n  // Normal result path\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre  = need_right ? (lz_eq_11 ? prod[10:0] : d4[11:1]) : prod[10:0];\n  wire              guard     = need_right ? (lz_eq_11 ? 1'b0 : d4[0]) : 1'b0;\n  wire              sticky    = need_right ? (lz_eq_11 ? 1'b0 : s4) : 1'b0;\n  wire              round_up  = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // Subnormal result path (uses the same d4/s4 when underflow is selected)\n  wire [9:0] frac_trunc_s = d4[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s    = d4[0];\n  wire       sticky_s   = s4;\n  wire       round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc_s[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc_s} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

[USER]
The design session is now over. Final result: PASS, best cost: 1.438e+05 area_delay_product.

Please write a brief summary covering:
1. What approaches did you try and what worked best?
2. What optimizations had the most impact?
3. What didn't work or caused regressions?
4. Lessons learned and what you would do differently next time.

Your design files are carried over to the next agent, so refer to them by filename where useful.
Be concise and specific.

[ASSISTANT]
### 1. Approaches tried and what worked best
- **Baseline:** `starting_point.v` was correct and gave **145,429** area-delay-product at default target.
- Tried operand-level LZ/pre-normalization (`fp_mul_v2/v3/v4`), tree LZ (`fp_mul_v5`), and shared/normal-subnormal barrel-shifter variants (`fp_mul_v7/v8/v9`).
- **Best working design:** an earlier version of `fp_mul_v6.sv`. It kept the basic unpack/multiply/classify structure but replaced the prefix-OR/mux `bit_at()` guard/sticky extraction with **logarithmic 22-bit right-shifters plus sticky accumulation**.
- Best measured result:
  - **area_delay_product: 143,841.665**
  - delay: 1359.8461
  - area: 105.7779
  - default `target_delay = 500`

### 2. Optimizations with the most impact
- Replacing the wide `below[k] = |prod[k:0]` prefix ORs and parameterized `bit_at()` mux chains with **staged logarithmic barrel shifters**:
  - stages of 1, 2, 4, 8, 16-bit shifts
  - guard bit comes from shifted product bit 0
  - sticky bit comes from ORing the bits shifted out in each stage
- Fixing the subnormal path to shift by **`q-1`**, where `q = 26 - exp_sum`, made the subnormal barrel path exact while reusing the same style of hardware.
- Leaving `target_delay` at 500 gave the best product. Higher targets reduced area slightly but increased delay enough to make the product worse.

### 3. What didn’t work or caused regressions
- Using `casez`/don’t-care patterns caused lint failure (`fp_mul_v2` initial version).
- Sharing one shifter between normal and subnormal paths failed correctness (`fp_mul_v8`).
- The first barrel-shifter subnormal version shifted by `q` instead of `q-1`, causing many subnormal errors until corrected.
- Input pre-normalization (`fp_mul_v4`) was correct but worsened cost substantially.
- Removing the variable left-shift path in `fp_mul_v7` actually regressed cost slightly.
- Secondary `target_delay` sweeps mostly worsened the area-delay product.

### 4. Lessons learned / next time
- Preserve the exact best file before making further edits. The best `fp_mul_v6.sv` state was overwritten by a later, slightly worse edit.
- In this datapath, the rounding guard/sticky extraction dominated area and delay; barrel-shifter-based sticky computation was the key improvement.
- Subnormal rounding arithmetic is very easy to break; any change to shift amount or carry handling must be evaluated immediately.
- Target-delay tuning was less valuable than datapath restructuring.
- Next steps should likely reconstruct the best `fp_mul_v6.sv` state by restoring the original `mant_pre` false branch to use the left-shifted path (`lshifted[10:0]`), then explore further reducing the multiplier/barrel path or improving the LZ generation.

================================================================================
